]
,
V(\nu_{Z})\subset S_{\mathbb{C}}
is defined over
\bar{k}
.
∎

6
Algebraicity of the zero locus of etale normal functions and the locus of Hodge Tate classes

Let
k
a field of finite type over
\mathbb{Q}
.
Let
f:X\to S
a smooth proper morphism with
S,X\in\mathop{\rm SmVar}\nolimits(k)
connected. Let
p
a prime number.
Let
Z\in\mathcal{Z}^{d}(X,n)^{\partial=0,f}_{hom}
.
By the definition, we have

V(\nu_{Z}^{et,p})\subset\cap_{l\in\mathbb{N},l\mbox{prime}}\cap_{\sigma_{l}:k\hookrightarrow\mathbb{C}_{l}}.V(\nu_{Z,\sigma_{l}}^{et,p})\subset S_{(0)}

and

V_{tors}(\nu_{Z}^{et,p})\subset\cap_{l\in\mathbb{N},l\mbox{prime}}\cap_{\sigma_{l}:k\hookrightarrow\mathbb{C}_{l}}.V_{tors}(\nu_{Z,\sigma_{l}}^{et,p})\subset S_{(0)}.

In this section, we investigate the algebraicity of
V_{tors}(\nu_{Z}^{et,p})\subset S

and of
V_{tors}(\nu_{Z,\sigma_{l}}^{et,p})\subset S
,
\sigma_{l}:k\hookrightarrow\mathbb{C}_{l}
.
Since
Z
is defined over
k
, we expect that the inclusion

V_{tors}(\nu_{Z}^{et,p})\subset V(\nu_{Z,\sigma_{l}}^{et,p})
is an equality for all primes
l\in\mathbb{N}
.

Remark 4
.

Let
k
a field of finite type over
\mathbb{Q}
.

[

We don’t need this result so we don’t give the details.

Definition 8
.

Let

a field of finite type over

Recall (see section 2) that

Go on by induction.

Lemma 3
.

Let
G
be a group. Consider a commutative diagram of
G
modules

\textstyle{0\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\textstyle{W\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{\pi^{*}}
\textstyle{V\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{\partial}
\scriptstyle{\pi^{*}}
\textstyle{K\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{\pi^{*}}
\textstyle{0}
\textstyle{0\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\textstyle{W^{\prime}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\textstyle{V^{\prime}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\scriptstyle{\partial^{\prime}}
\textstyle{K^{\prime}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}
\textstyle{0}

whose rows are exact sequence and
\pi^{*}:V\to V^{\prime}
is injective.
Let
\alpha\in V
.
Then
\alpha\in V^{G}
and
\partial\alpha\neq 0
if and only if
\pi^{*}\alpha\in V^{{}^{\prime}G}
and
\partial^{\prime}\pi^{*}\alpha\neq 0
.

Proof.

Follows from the fact that
<\alpha>
define a splitting
W\oplus<\alpha>\subset V
of
G
modules.
∎

Theorem 7
.

Let
k
a field of finite type over
\mathbb{Q}
.
Let
f:X\to S
a smooth proper morphism with
S,X\in\mathop{\rm SmVar}\nolimits(k)
connected.
Let
p\in\mathbb{N}\backslash\delta(S,X/S)
a be prime number,
where
\delta(S,X/S)
is the finite set given in definition
8
.
Let
\sigma_{p}:k\hookrightarrow\mathbb{C}_{p}
be an embedding.
Consider
\hat{k}_{\sigma_{p}}\subset\mathbb{C}_{p}
the
p
adic completion of
k
with respect to
\sigma_{p}
.
Let
Z\in\mathcal{Z}^{d}(X,n)^{\partial=0,f}_{hom}
.

(i)

We have

V_{tors}(\nu_{Z}^{et,p})=T\cap S_{(0)}\subset S,

where
T\subset S
is given in definition
8
.

(ii)

For each embedding
\sigma_{p}:k\hookrightarrow\mathbb{C}_{p}
, we have

V_{tors}(\nu_{Z,\sigma_{p}}^{et,p})_{\hat{k}_{\sigma_{p}}}=\hat{T}_{\sigma_{p}}\cap S_{(0),\hat{k}_{\sigma_{p}}}\subset S_{\hat{k}_{\sigma_{p}}},

where
\hat{T}_{\sigma_{p}}\subset S_{\hat{k}_{\sigma_{p}}}
is given in definition
8
,
and for
V\subset S
a subset,
V_{\hat{k}_{\sigma_{p}}}:=\pi_{k/\hat{k}_{\sigma_{p}}}(S)^{-1}(V)
,
where
\pi_{k/\hat{k}_{\sigma_{p}}}(S):S_{\hat{k}_{\sigma_{p}}}\to S
being the projection.

Proof.

Let
\sigma_{p}:k\hookrightarrow\mathbb{C}_{p}
be an embedding.
For each
\alpha\in\Lambda
, by the semi-stable comparaison theorem for

f^{\alpha}:=f\circ\pi^{\alpha}:((X_{S^{\alpha}}\times\square^{n})_{\hat{k}_{\sigma_{p}}}^{\alpha,\mathcal{O}},N^{\mathcal{O}}_{U^{\alpha}})\to(S^{\alpha,\mathcal{O}}_{\hat{k}_{\sigma_{p}}},N_{\mathcal{O}})

(
[
1
]
) which is log smooth,
we have the isomorphism in
\mathop{\rm Shv}\nolimits_{fil,G,\phi_{p},N}(S^{\alpha}_{\hat{k}_{\sigma_{p}}})

\displaystyle H^{j}f^{\alpha}_{*}\alpha(U^{\alpha}):R^{j}f^{\alpha}_{*}\mathbb{Z}_{p,U^{\alpha,et}_{\mathbb{C}_{p}}}\otimes_{\mathbb{Z}_{p}}\mathbb{B}_{st,S^{\alpha}_{\hat{k}_{\sigma_{p}}}}\xrightarrow{\sim}H^{j}\int_{f^{\alpha}}j_{*Hdg}(O_{U^{\alpha}_{\hat{k}_{\sigma_{p}}}},F_{b})\otimes_{O_{S_{\hat{k}_{\sigma_{p}}}}}O\mathbb{B}_{st,S^{\alpha}_{\hat{k}_{\sigma_{p}}}},

(2)

recall that since
p\in\mathbb{N}\backslash\delta(S)
,

\hat{k}_{\sigma_{p}}(s)
is unramified for all
s\in S_{\hat{k}_{\sigma_{p}}}
.

(i):
Let
s\in S^{\alpha}_{(0)}
. Denote
G:=Gal(\bar{k}/k(s))
.

•

The map
\pi_{s}^{\alpha}:(X_{s}\times\square^{n})^{\alpha}\to X_{s}\times\square^{n}
is generically finite
since
\pi^{\alpha}:(X\times\square^{n})^{\alpha}\to X\times\square^{n}
is generically finite and
f
is flat.
Thus by lemma
3
and theorem
4
,
\nu^{et,p}_{Z}(s):=AJ^{et,p}(X_{s})(Z_{s})=0

if and only if there exists

\alpha\in H^{2d-1}_{et}(U_{S^{\alpha},s},\mathbb{Z}_{p})^{[Z_{s}]}

such that
\pi^{\alpha,-1}(\alpha)\in H^{2d-1}_{et}(U^{\alpha}_{s},\mathbb{Z}_{p})(d)^{G}
and

\partial\pi^{\alpha,-1}(\alpha)\neq 0
.

•

On the other hand by (
2
) and proposition
5
(ii),

\alpha^{\prime}\in H^{2d-1}_{et}(U^{\alpha}_{s},\mathbb{Z}_{p})(d)^{G}
if and only if (see definition
2
)

w(\alpha^{\prime})_{k}\in F^{d}H_{DR}^{2d-1}(U^{\alpha}_{s})
and

w(\alpha^{\prime})\in(H_{DR}^{2d-1}(U^{\alpha}_{s,\hat{k}_{\sigma_{p}}})\otimes_{\hat{k}_{\sigma_{p}}(s)}\mathbb{B}_{st})^{\phi_{p},N}.

Moreover
w(\pi^{\alpha,-1}(\alpha))=\pi^{\alpha,-1}(w(\alpha))
.

(ii): Let
s\in S_{(0)}^{\alpha}
and
s^{\prime}\in\pi_{k/\hat{k}_{\sigma_{p}}}(S)^{-1}(s)
.

•

Since
\pi_{s}^{\alpha}:(X_{s}\times\square^{n})^{\alpha}\to X_{s}\times\square^{n}
is generically finite (see the proof of (i))
we have by lemma
3
and theorem
5
,

\nu^{et,p}_{Z,\sigma_{p}}(s):=AJ^{et,p}_{\sigma_{p}}(X_{s})(Z_{s})=0
if and only if there exists

\alpha\in H^{2d-1}_{et}(U_{S^{\alpha},s,\hat{k}_{\sigma_{p}}},\mathbb{Z}_{p})^{[Z_{s}]}

such that

\pi^{\alpha,-1}(\alpha)\in H^{2d-1}_{et}(U^{\alpha}_{s,\hat{k}_{\sigma_{p}}},\mathbb{Z}_{p})(d)^{\hat{G}_{\sigma_{p}}}

and
\partial\alpha\neq 0
.

•

On the other hand by (
2
),

\alpha^{\prime}\in H^{2d-1}_{et}(U^{\alpha}_{s,\hat{k}_{\sigma_{p}}},\mathbb{Z}_{p})(d)^{\hat{G}_{\sigma_{p}}}
if and only if
(see definition
2
)
w(\alpha^{\prime})\in F^{d}H_{DR}^{2d-1}(U^{\alpha}_{\hat{k}_{\sigma_{p}},s})
and

w(\alpha^{\prime})\in(H_{DR}^{2d-1}(U^{\alpha}_{s,\hat{k}_{\sigma_{p}}})\otimes_{\hat{k}_{\sigma_{p}}(s)}\mathbb{B}_{st})^{\phi_{p},N}=(H_{DR}^{2d-1}(U^{\alpha}_{s^{\prime},\hat{k}_{\sigma_{p}}})\otimes_{\hat{k}_{\sigma_{p}}(s)}\mathbb{B}_{st})^{\phi_{p},N}.

Moreover
w(\pi^{\alpha,-1}(\alpha))=\pi^{\alpha,-1}(w(\alpha))
.

∎

Proposition
1
for (i), the main result of
[
1
]

together with proposition
5
for (ii), gives the following :

Theorem 8
.

Let
f:X\to S
be a smooth morphism, with
S,X\in\mathop{\rm SmVar}\nolimits(k)

over a field
k\subset\mathbb{C}
of finite type over
\mathbb{Q}
.
Take a compactification
f:X\xrightarrow{j}\bar{X}\xrightarrow{\bar{f}}S
of
f
where
\bar{X}\in\mathop{\rm SmVar}\nolimits(k)
,

j
is an open embedding and
\bar{f}
is projective. Denote
Z:=\bar{X}\backslash X
. Consider

E^{j}_{DR}(X/S):=H^{j}\int_{\bar{f}}j_{*Hdg}(O_{X},F_{b})\in DRM(S),

see
[
5
]
for the definition of the category of De Rham modules.
Let
p\in\mathbb{N}\backslash\delta(S,\bar{X}/S)
a prime number and
\sigma_{p}:k\hookrightarrow\mathbb{C}_{p}
an embedding.
Denote
\hat{k}_{\sigma_{p}}\subset\mathbb{C}_{p}
the
p
-adic completion of
k
with respect to
\sigma_{p}
.
Consider, using definition
8
, a stratification
S=\sqcup_{\alpha\in\Lambda}S^{\alpha}
,

\Lambda
being a finite set, by locally closed subset
S^{\alpha}\subset S
, and alterations
(i.e. generically finite morphisms)
\pi^{\alpha}:X^{\alpha}\to X_{S^{\alpha}}
such that

f_{\alpha}:=f\circ\pi^{\alpha}:(\bar{X}_{\hat{k}_{\sigma_{p}}}^{\alpha,\mathcal{O}},N_{Z,\mathcal{O}})\to(S_{\hat{k}_{\sigma_{p}}}^{\alpha},N_{\mathcal{O}})

is log smooth. Consider for
j,d\in\mathbb{Z}
,

•

the locus of Hodge Tate classes

HT^{p}_{j,d}(X/S):=(\sqcup_{\alpha\in\Lambda}(E^{j}_{DR}(X_{\hat{k}_{\sigma_{p}}}^{\alpha}/S_{\hat{k}_{\sigma_{p}}}^{\alpha})\otimes_{O_{X_{\hat{k}_{\sigma_{p}}}^{\alpha}}}O\mathbb{B}_{st,X})^{\phi_{p},N})\cap F^{d}E^{j}_{DR}(X_{\hat{k}_{\sigma_{p}}}/S_{\hat{k}_{\sigma_{p}}})\subset E^{j}_{DR}(X_{\hat{k}_{\sigma_{p}}}/S_{\hat{k}_{\sigma_{p}}})

where

E^{j}_{DR}(X_{\hat{k}_{\sigma_{p}}}/S_{\hat{k}_{\sigma_{p}}}):=H^{j}\int_{\bar{f}}j_{*Hdg}(O_{X_{\hat{k}_{\sigma_{p}}}},F_{b})=E^{j}_{DR}(X/S)\otimes_{k}\hat{k}_{\sigma_{p}}\in DRM(S_{\hat{k}_{\sigma_{p}}}),

•

the locus of Hodge classes

HL_{j,d}(X/S):=HL_{j,d}(X_{\mathbb{C}}/S_{\mathbb{C}}):=F^{d}E^{j}_{DR}(X_{\mathbb{C}}/S_{\mathbb{C}})\cap R^{j}f_{*}\mathbb{Q}_{X_{\mathbb{C}}^{an}}\subset E^{j}_{DR}(X_{\mathbb{C}}/S_{\mathbb{C}})

where

E^{j}_{DR}(X_{\mathbb{C}}/S_{\mathbb{C}}):=H^{j}\int_{f}(O_{X_{\mathbb{C}}},F_{b})=E^{j}_{DR}(X/S)\otimes_{k}\mathbb{C}\in DRM(S_{\mathbb{C}}).

Then,

(i)

for each subfield
k^{\prime}\subset\mathbb{C}
, we have a canonical embedding

\displaystyle ev(X/S):HT^{p}_{j,d}(X/S)(k^{\prime})\hookrightarrow HL_{j,d}(X_{\mathbb{C}}/S_{\mathbb{C}})\otimes_{\mathbb{Q}}\mathbb{Q}_{p},

\displaystyle w_{s}\mapsto(1/2i\pi)ev(X/S)(w_{s}):=ev(X_{s})(w_{s}),\,s\in S_{k^{\prime}},

note that the image consists of logarithmic classes, hence

p_{S_{\hat{k}_{\sigma_{p}}}}(HT^{p}_{j,d}(X/S))(k^{\prime})\subset p_{S_{\mathbb{C}}}(HL_{j,d}(X_{\mathbb{C}}/S_{\mathbb{C}})),

where
p_{S_{\mathbb{C}}}:E^{j}_{DR}(X_{\mathbb{C}}/S_{\mathbb{C}})\to S_{\mathbb{C}}
and

p_{S_{\hat{k}_{\sigma_{p}}}}:E^{j}_{DR}(X_{\hat{k}_{\sigma_{p}}}/S_{\hat{k}_{\sigma_{p}}})\to S_{\hat{k}_{\sigma_{p}}}

are the projections,

(ii)

we have

(R^{j}f_{*}\mathbb{Q}_{p,X_{\bar{k}}^{et}}(d))^{G}=<HT^{p}_{j,d}(X/S)\cap F^{d}E^{j}_{DR}(X/S)>_{\mathbb{Q}_{p}}\subset E^{j}_{DR}(X_{\hat{k}_{\sigma_{p}}}/S_{\hat{k}_{\sigma_{p}}}),

where
<->_{\mathbb{Q}_{p}}
denote the
\mathbb{Q}_{p}
subvector bundle generated by
(-)
,

(iii)

we have a canonical embedding in
\mathop{\rm Shv}\nolimits(S_{\mathbb{C}})

\displaystyle ev(X/S):\pi_{k/\mathbb{C}}(S)^{*}(R^{j}f_{*}\mathbb{Q}_{p,X_{\bar{k}}^{et}}(d))^{G}\hookrightarrow HL_{j,d}(X_{\mathbb{C}}/S_{\mathbb{C}})\otimes_{\mathbb{Q}}\mathbb{Q}_{p},

\displaystyle\alpha_{s}\mapsto(1/2i\pi)ev(X/S)(w(\alpha_{s})):=ev(X_{k(s)})((1/2i\pi)w(\alpha_{s})),\,s^{\prime}=\pi_{k/\mathbb{C}}^{-1}(s):k(s)\hookrightarrow\mathbb{C},\,s\in S,

where
\pi_{k/\mathbb{C}}(S):S_{\mathbb{C}}\to S
is the projection.

Proof.

(i): Follows from proposition
1
and the equality, by (the proof of) proposition
4
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{H}^{j}(X_{s,\mathbb{C}_{p}},\Omega^{\bullet\geq d}_{\bar{X}^{\mathcal{O}}_{s,\hat{k}^{\prime}_{\sigma_{p}}}}(\mathop{\rm log}\nolimits D^{\mathcal{O}}_{s,\hat{k}^{\prime}_{\sigma_{p}}})\otimes_{O_{X_{s}^{\mathcal{O}}}}O\mathbb{B}_{st,X_{s,\hat{k}^{\prime}_{\sigma_{p}}},\mathop{\rm log}\nolimits D_{\hat{k}^{\prime}_{\sigma_{p}}}})^{\phi_{p},N,\hat{G}}=\mathbb{H}_{pet}^{j}(X_{s,\mathbb{C}_{p}},\Omega^{\bullet\geq l}_{X_{s,\mathbb{C}_{p}},\mathop{\rm log}\nolimits,\mathcal{O}}\otimes\mathbb{Z}_{p}).

where
(\bar{X}^{\prime}_{s},D_{s})\to(\bar{X}_{s},Z_{s})
is a desingularization,
i.e.
\bar{X}^{\prime}_{s}
is smooth and
D_{s}\subset\bar{X}^{\prime}_{s}
is a normal crossing divisor,
after taking an embedding
\sigma^{\prime}_{p}:k^{\prime}\hookrightarrow\mathbb{C}_{p}
.

(ii):Follows from the isomorphism
(H^{j}f^{\alpha}_{*}\alpha(X^{\alpha}))
(c.f. theorem
[
1
]
)
and proposition
5
(ii).

(iii):Follows from (i) and (ii).
∎
